## Typical Workflow

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/VIZIGO\_workflow\_EN.xml

1. Prepare the sheath according to the sheath's IFU.
2. Access vasculature using your common clinical practice.
3. Connect the sheath to the cable, and then the cable to the PIU.
4. Connect the supported ablation catheter to the MAP socket.
5. Select sheath visualization settings, if needed.
6. Insert compatible catheter through sheath.
7. Build the matrix with a sensor-based catheter (therapeutic or diagnostic) to enable sheath visualization.

The sheath is visualized as soon as the matrix is built.

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![DxNoteGeneric.png](data:image/png;base64...) | NOTE  Visualization of the CARTO VIZIGO™ Sheath is not intended for use for visualizing transseptal puncture or monitoring the sheath in the peripheral veins. |

### Pairing or Unpairing the Sheath

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/VIZIGO\_unpairing\_EN.xml

When the system detects that a CARTO VIZIGO™ Sheath and a supported sensor-based focal ablation catheter are both connected, the System automatically pairs them.

If that catheter is not the catheter in the CARTO VIZIGO™ Sheath, the sheath and catheter must be unpaired (Paired with set to None).

In some situations, such as reconnecting a supported catheter after manually unpairing it, pairing must be set manually (Paired with set to MAP).

Table 69: Options for Pairing or Unpairing

| Option | Example |
| --- | --- |
| In the Catheter Setup Screen, set the Paired with option. | ![cath_setup_vizigo_small.png](data:image/png;base64...) |
| Right-click the ![setup_cath_btn_v7.png](data:image/png;base64...) button. Select the sheath and set the Paired with option. | ![VIZIGO_cath_list_v7.png](data:image/png;base64...) |
| Right-click the sheath visualization and set the Paired with option. | ![vizigo_sheath_none_v7p3.png](data:image/png;base64...) |

The sheath can be paired or unpaired through the same options.

### Troubleshooting CARTO VIZIGO™ Sheath Visualization

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/VIZIGO\_troubleshooting\_EN.xml

Training

| Problem | Actions |
| --- | --- |
| Sheath is not visualized | Try the following actions until the issue is resolved:   * Ensure that Sheath Image is checked in the Catheter Setup Screen. * Click Help > Show Visualization Matrix. If the matrix is incomplete, add more data. (Rotate the map viewer to view the matrix in other orientations.) |
| Stripes appear on catheter or sheath | Stripes indicate reduced accuracy.  Try the following actions until the issue is resolved:   * Add matrix data.   If the sheath is partially in the right atrium (RA), add matrix data in the RA near the septum.   * Check for patch movement. * Check for magnetic interference. |
| Break between sheath and catheter visualization when catheter tip is far from sheath | This is standard system behavior. The break disappears when the catheter is drawn back into the sheath.  DOR chart, 8  See ["Interpolation Limit"](#target9000084). |
| Break between sheath and catheter visualization, but catheter is close to sheath | Try the following actions until the issue is resolved:   * Add matrix data. * Reset sheath pairing: click Catheter > Reset Sheath Pairing |
| Catheter and sheath are not immediately visualized after reconnecting catheter | If the shaft between sheath and catheter tip was in a complex curve (for example, S-curve or loop) when the catheter was disconnected, Both the catheter and the sheath visualization disappear. They are unpaired.  When the catheter is reconnected, there may be a long delay before visualization is restored. When it is restored, pairing and sheath visualization are reset as expected.  DOR chart, 10, 10.1 |
| Appearance of shaft between sheath and catheter tip is distorted or not logical | * If catheter in sheath is supported for paired visualization, reset sheath pairing. Click Catheter > Reset Sheath Pairing * If catheter in the sheath is not supported for paired visualization, ensure that the sheath is unpaired.   See ["Pairing or Unpairing the Sheath"](#target9000085). |

See also:

* ["Other Visual Indications"](#target9000086)

# CARTO PRIME™ Module

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/Prime\_modules\_EN.xml

## CARTO PRIME™ Module Overview

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/prime\_modules\_overview\_EN.xml

The CARTO PRIME™ Module combines the following advanced mapping features:

* The **CARTOFINDER™** Module provides automatic analysis of multiple, simultaneous intracardiac (IC) signals and enables the creation of high density dynamic maps.

See ["CARTOFINDER™ Module"](#target9000087).

* **Coherent Mapping** is a CARTO™ 3 System feature that improves the representation of the electric wave propagation over the atria by means of coloring and direction vectors, focusing on the displayed path of cyclic arrhythmia propagation. The reconstruction includes detection of areas with probable conduction barriers (slow or no conduction).

See ["COHERENT Mapping"](#target9000088).

* **Parallel Mapping** can be used for continuous mapping of up to 4 maps with different map settings and different acquisition filters. For example, different acquisition filters can be used for different settings, such as cycle length, signal morphology, etc. In addition, acquired data can be recalculated retrospectively according to different acquisition filters.

See ["Parallel Mapping"](#target9000089).

* **LAT Hybrid** maps provide a solution for PVC mapping by creating a hybrid point: the coloring reflects the LAT value during PVC rhythm, and the location corresponds to the location during sinus rhythm.

See ["LAT Hybrid Maps"](#target9000090).

* **Map Merge** is an enhanced way to load maps from a previous study with VISITAG™ and ablation data, and view them together with current study maps. Using maps from previous studies in a current active map allows you to navigate the catheter to areas of interest from previous ablations and identify areas where the study may need to be re-evaluated.

See ["Map Merge"](#target9000091).

### CARTO PRIME™ Module Installation

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/prime\_modules\_installation\_EN.xml

The CARTO PRIME™ Module requires a CARTO PRIME™ Workstation.

A Biosense Webster representative is responsible for the installation and initial configuration of the module.

## CARTOFINDER™ Module

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/CFinder\_intro\_v7\_EN.xml

The **CARTOFINDER™** Module provides automatic analysis of multiple, simultaneous intracardiac (IC) signals and enables the creation of high density dynamic maps.

The CARTOFINDER™ algorithm analyzes the recorded ECG data and calculates annotations on the unipolar IC signals, allowing the detection and display of electrical activation patterns in atrial arrhythmias in a dynamic fashion. The CARTOFINDER™ Module expands the CARTO™ 3 System mapping capabilities to include irregular activation and possible identification of repetitive focal and rotational activation patterns. When one of the CARTOFINDER™ map coloring types is selected, the CARTOFINDER™ sites contribute to the coloring on the map surface.

V7 REP9626\_1.4: 1.5.8

The CARTOFINDER™ Module uses input from a PENTARAY™ NAV Catheter connected to the PIU.

FDA Request

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![DxNoteCaution.png](data:image/png;base64...) | *CAUTION*   * The information provided by these maps is for diagnostic purposes only. Therapeutic treatment should rely on all available information in the EP lab. * The CARTOFINDER™ algorithm is not designed to detect complex activation patterns in AF (unstable or meandering rotors, wavefront collision). |

V7 REP9626\_1.4: 1.5.5, 1.5.6, 1.5.8, 1.5.14, 1.5.17

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![DxNoteDanger.png](data:image/png;base64...) | **WARNING**  Before applying energy for ablation, visually verify the catheter tip position in relation to the map displayed on the monitor and confirm the accuracy of the signals and annotations. Use your common clinical practice for real-time verification of catheter locations throughout the procedure (inspection of IC signals, direct imaging guidance such as fluoroscopy or ultrasound, etc.). Failure to do so might result in incorrect placement of the catheter. |

### Supported Catheters

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/CARTOFINDERCatheters\_v7\_EN.xml

CARTOFINDER™ recordings are available when using a catheter from the PENTARAY™ NAV Catheter family as the mapping catheter.

Contact your Biosense Webster representative for details of compatible catheters that are available in your country.

### Working with the CARTOFINDER™ Module

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/PeformStudy\_title\_EN.xml

#### CARTOFINDER™ Module Preferences

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/CARTOFINDERPreferences\_V7P3\_EN.xml

User-defined preferences can be applied when the CARTOFINDER™ Module is active. These settings are accessed through the CARTOFINDER™ tab of the Preferences dialog box (Tools > Preferences).

| Option | Description |
| --- | --- |
| Automatically Acquire EA Points | Automatically acquire EA points from the mapping catheter when CARTOFINDER™ recording starts.  This option is cleared by default. |
| Discard Low Quality Signals | Filter out low-quality signals to ensure that only high-quality signals participate in map coloring.  This option is selected by default.  Low-quality signals may be affected by any of the following conditions:   * Poor tissue contact * Severely scarred tissue * Low amplitude |
| CL mapping filter: Set the max CL Std (ms) | Set the standard deviation filter thresholds (ms) for CARTOFINDER™ CL maps. |

#### Recording CARTOFINDER™ Module Data

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/CARTOFINDERInterface\_v7\_EN.xml

The CARTOFINDER™ recording button in the Acquisition Panel is hidden by default. To enable CARTOFINDER™ recording, click Tools > CARTOFINDER™ Module On. The recording button is added to the Acquisition Panel. (This does not affect other CARTOFINDER™ Module options. They are always available if the CARTO PRIME™ Module is installed.)

The recording button is disabled during the following situations:

* No supported catheter is connected to the system
* A supported catheter is not selected as the mapping catheter
* The system is in an error state
* There is a disconnection in the body surface electrodes
* FAM acquisition is active

During CARTOFINDER™ recording, you cannot perform the following actions:

* New Map
* Remap
* Save Map As
* Map Replay
* Copy Point To or Move Point To
* FAM acquisition or continuous EA acquisition

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| 1. | In the Map Setup screen, set the mapping catheter to a supported catheter. |
| 2. | Click the CARTOFINDER™ recording button in the Acquisition Panel to begin recording. Keep the mapping catheter stable during recording.  ![CARTOFINDER_AcquisitionPanel_RecordingButton.png](data:image/jpeg;base64...)  During recording, the button is highlighted in orange. The button displays the recording duration (in seconds).  ![CARTOFINDER_AcquisitionPanel_DuringRecording.png](data:image/png;base64...)  The CARTOFINDER™ recording stops automatically after 30 seconds.   |  |  | | --- | --- | | ![DxNoteGeneric.png](data:image/png;base64...) | NOTE  It is recommended to record for at least 20 seconds. | |
| 3. | To stop the recording before 30 seconds, click the CARTOFINDER™ button.   |  |  | | --- | --- | | ![DxNoteGeneric.png](data:image/png;base64...) | NOTE  During the first 10 seconds of recording, there is a red X on the recording button. If you stop the recording before 10 seconds, no CARTOFINDER™ site is created.  ![CARTOFINDER_AcquisitionPanel_10Seconds.png](data:image/png;base64...) | |

When the CARTOFINDER™ recording ends (either automatically or by clicking on the recording button), the recording button turns blue and the recording duration disappears.

You can perform multiple recordings consecutively on the same anatomical map, even while the algorithm is analyzing previous recordings.

Certain situations cause the recording to stop automatically:

* Catheter disconnection
* Body surface electrode disconnection
* Changing the active map
* Changing to an unsupported mapping catheter
* System errors, including map shift
  + If an error occurs before the 10-second minimum time has been reached, the recording stops and no CARTOFINDER™ site is created.
  + If an error occurs after the 10-second minimum time, the recording stops and a CARTOFINDER™ site is created.

V7 REP9626\_1.4: 1.5.3

The CARTOFINDER™ algorithm processes the recorded data, including the mapping catheter used, catheter position, ECG channels used, recording start and end time, stimulation time and channels.

#### Viewing CARTOFINDER™ Sites

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/CARTOFINDERViewingSites\_v7\_EN.xml

The algorithm receives data from the recording and detects CARTOFINDER™ sites. A CARTOFINDER™ site, which is a collection of ECG data acquired during a CARTOFINDER™ recording at the average location of the mapping catheter during recording time, is added on top of the map reconstruction.

A CARTOFINDER™ site in the map functions similarly to a point or contour. Some examples include:

* Selecting a CARTOFINDER™ site in the Main Map Viewer selects the CARTOFINDER™ site in the Point List, Selected Point Viewer, and the Additional Map Viewer (if the same map is active in both map viewers).
* Selecting a CARTOFINDER™ site in the Point List results in display of the CARTOFINDER™ site's signals in the Selected Point Viewer.
* A CARTOFINDER™ site can be deleted, copied, moved, or restored from the map.

A CARTOFINDER™ site is electrical only and does not contribute to the map reconstruction. A CARTOFINDER™ site only contributes to the map coloring of a CARTOFINDER™ Module map.

| Display | Description |

| ![CARTOFINDER_Site_Selected.png](data:image/png;base64...) | The center of a CARTOFINDER™ site is displayed as a ring-shaped tag.   * When selected, the tag is highlighted with a white ring around it and its projected electrodes are displayed * When the calculation is complete, the tag is white   CARTOFINDER™ site electrodes are displayed as rectangles around the center of a CARTOFINDER™ site. They are white if no region of interest (ROI) is detected |
| ![CARTOFINDER_CalculatingFinderSites.png](data:image/png;base64...) | The center of the CARTOFINDER™ site and its electrodes are gray during algorithm calculation. Progress is displayed on the center of the site as it changes from gray to white. |
| ![CARTOFINDER_Site_striped.png](data:image/png;base64...) | Stripes indicate a CARTOFINDER™ site that is unstable due to catheter movement exceeding the threshold during recording. |
| ![CARTOFINDER_Electrodes_green_SiteNotSelected.png](data:image/png;base64...) | * Electrodes where focal or rotational activity was detected are displayed by default, even when the CARTOFINDER™ site is not selected. (For more information, see ["Hiding or Displaying CARTOFINDER™ Sites and ROI Electrodes in the Map"](#target9000092).) * When you select an ROI electrode, the corresponding site becomes selected (highlighted with a white ring around the center). |

| ![CARTOFINDER_Electrodes_green.png](data:image/png;base64...) | After algorithm calculation, electrodes with detected focal activity are green and enlarged.  Focal activity refers to a repetitive activation pattern that demonstrates early consecutive QS morphology.  (For more information on the different types of green map coloring and their indications, see ["CARTOFINDER™ Map Coloring"](#target9000093).) |

Rotational activity refers to repetitive activation pattern that demonstrates rotational activation. ##### Hiding or Displaying CARTOFINDER™ Sites and ROI Electrodes in the Map

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/CARTOFINDERVisualizationSetup\_v7\_EN.xml

You can hide or display CARTOFINDER™ sites and ROI electrodes in the map. You can define different settings for each map, in each map viewer.

To open the dialog box, right-click the map and select Visualization Setup.

![CARTOFINDER_VisualizationSetupDialogBox.png](data:image/png;base64...)

Figure 136: Visualization Setup Dialog Box with CARTOFINDER™ Options

Table 71: CARTOFINDER™ Options in the Visualization Setup Dialog Box

| Option | Description |
| --- | --- |
| Site Indications | When selected, CARTOFINDER™ sites and projected electrodes are displayed.  When cleared, CARTOFINDER™ sites and projected electrodes are hidden.  This option is selected by default. |
| All ROI electrodes | When selected, all ROI electrodes are displayed, even for CARTOFINDER™ sites that are not selected.  When cleared, ROI electrodes are only displayed for CARTOFINDER™ sites that are selected.  This option is selected by default. |

Selecting Apply to All Maps applies the selected options to all maps in the study.

##### CARTOFINDER™ Sites in the Point List

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/CARTOFINDERSitePointList\_v7\_EN.xml

When a CARTOFINDER™ site is assigned to a map, it is added to the Point List.

![CARTOFINDER_PointList.png](data:image/png;base64...)

Figure 137: Point List for CARTOFINDER™ Module Maps

The following table describes columns in the Point List that display information related to CARTOFINDER™ Module maps.

Table 72: Point List Columns in CARTOFINDER™ Module Maps

| Data | Description |
| --- | --- |
| Number | Displays the sequential number assigned to each point, contour, or CARTOFINDER™ site. |
| Type | Displays the data type (point, contour, or CARTOFINDER™ site).  The CARTOFINDER™ site includes an indication to differentiate it from a point or contour:   |  |  | | --- | --- | | ![CARTOFINDER_Site_PointListIndication.png](data:image/png;base64...) | Stable CARTOFINDER™ site | | ![CARTOFINDER_Site_PointListUnstableIndication.png](data:image/png;base64...) | Unstable CARTOFINDER™ site | |
| LAT | Displays the LAT value of a point.  For a CARTOFINDER™ site, no LAT value is displayed. |
| CL | Displays the cycle length of the point, contour, or CARTOFINDER™ site.  For a CARTOFINDER™ site, this value represents the dominant CL of all electrodes during the recording time. |
| Time | Displays the time at which the point or contour was acquired  For a CARTOFINDER™ site, the time at the beginning of the CARTOFINDER™ site recording is displayed. |
| Focal | Total number of focal events identified on all electrodes of the CARTOFINDER™ site throughout the recording. |
| Rotational | Total number of rotational events identified on all electrodes of the CARTOFINDER™ site throughout the recording. |
| Duration | Displays the duration of CARTOFINDER™ site recording. |
| Comment | Displays any comments assigned to the point, contour, or CARTOFINDER™ site.  To add comments, double-click in the comments field. |

Columns that apply only to the CARTOFINDER™ Module are hidden when a different map coloring type is displayed.

##### CARTOFINDER™ Sites in the Selected Point Viewer

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/CARTOFINDERSite\_SPV\_v7\_EN.xml

The Selected Point Viewer includes all unipolar channels of the mapping catheter by default.

![CARTOFINDER_SPV_checkboxes.png](data:image/png;base64...)

Figure 138: CARTOFINDER™ Site Channels in the Selected Point Viewer

The following information is displayed for a selected channel: Site number, channel number, cycle length, cycle length variation, rotational index, and focal index.

![CARTOFINDER_SPV_details.png](data:image/png;base64...)

Figure 139: Channel Details in the Selected Point Viewer

A CARTOFINDER™ site's channels can be excluded or included using the check boxes. Excluded channels do not participate in map coloring. If you select or clear a channel, the algorithm recalculates the CARTOFINDER™ site. Excluded channels are grayed out in the Selected Point Viewer, and the corresponding electrodes are colored black in the map.

![CARTOFINDER_ECGViewer_SPV.png](data:image/png;base64...)

Figure 140: Example of Channel Selection for the CARTOFINDER™ Site

Each CARTOFINDER™ site has its own set of mapping catheter unipolar and bipolar channels that are displayed in the Selected Point Viewer. Changes to the displayed channels of one CARTOFINDER™ site do not affect the displayed channels for other CARTOFINDER™ sites. However, display of non-mapping channels, such as body surface channels, is the same for all CARTOFINDER™ sites. Adding or removing body surface channels for one CARTOFINDER™ site applies to all CARTOFINDER™ sites.

The channel selection in the Selected Point Viewer for points and for CARTOFINDER™ sites are separate. Changes made to the ECG signal display of CARTOFINDER™ sites don't affect points and vice versa.

To hide channels that were filtered out by the algorithm or that were filtered out manually by clearing their check boxes, click ![CARTOFINDER_ViewerOptionsMenu_Button.png](data:image/png;base64...) and select Hide Filtered CARTOFINDER™ Channels. The filtered-out channels are removed from the Selected Point Viewer.

![CARTOFINDER_ECGViewer_SPV_ViewerOptions_HideUnused.png](data:image/png;base64...)

Figure 141: Hide Filtered CARTOFINDER™ Channels Option

If multiple CARTOFINDER™ sites are selected, no ECG signals are displayed in the Selected Point Viewer.

When focal or rotational activity occurs, the corresponding time segments are displayed darker than the background color of the Selected Point Viewer.

![CARTOFINDER_ROIActivity_SPV.png](data:image/png;base64...)

Figure 142: Indication of Focal or Rotational Activity in the Selected Point Viewer

* To revert to the previous focal or rotational activity, click ![CARTOFINDER_site_scroll_prev.png](data:image/png;base64...) or press CTRL plus the **[** key.
* To move to the next focal or rotational activity, click ![CARTOFINDER_site_scroll_next.png](data:image/png;base64...) or press CTRL plus the **]** key.

When moving to the next or previous focal or rotational activity, the activity is displayed in the center of the Selected Point Viewer. The relevant channels, with their corresponding electrodes participating in the activity, are automatically selected.

#### Viewing a CARTOFINDER™ Module Map

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/CARTOFINDERSelectingFINDERColoring\_v7p3\_EN.xml

You can view CARTOFINDER™ Module maps in the Main Map Viewer and the Additional Map Viewer.

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| 1. | Right-click the map.  The Map Shortcut menu appears.  ![CARTOFINDER_maptype_coloring.png](data:image/png;base64...) |

The color scale bar changes to reflect the new map coloring type.

Table 73: Color Scale Bar with CARTOFINDER™ Module Maps

| CARTOFINDER™ Module Map Type | Color Scale Bar |
| --- | --- |
| CARTOFINDER™ | ![CARTOFINDER_ROIColoring_ColorBar.png](data:image/png;base64...) |
| CARTOFINDER™ Dynamic | ![CARTOFINDER_Dynamic_ColorBar.png](data:image/png;base64...) |

Map coloring in CARTOFINDER™ Module maps is based on the following considerations:

* CARTOFINDER™ sites contribute to the coloring and project color on the map surface
* Electroanatomical points do not contribute to map coloring
* Filtered-out electrodes do not contribute to map coloring
* CARTOFINDER™ sites do not contribute to map coloring if the distance between the site and the map reconstruction is greater than 30 mm.

The following options are disabled in CARTOFINDER™ Module maps:

* Isochronal Map
* Propagation
* Early Meets Late

##### CARTOFINDER™ Map Coloring

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/CARTOFINDERROI\_Coloring\_v7\_EN.xml

When the CARTOFINDER™ map is selected, map coloring is updated. The color scale bar changes.

![CARTOFINDER_ROIColoring_ColorBar.png](data:image/png;base64...)

Figure 143: CARTOFINDER™ Color Scale Bar

The color scale bar is divided according to intensity level of the focal or rotational activity.

* Blue indicates a rotational activation pattern:
  + Light blue: low intensity
  + Medium blue: medium intensity
  + Dark blue: high intensity
* White indicates no focal or rotational activation patterns.
* Green indicates a focal activation pattern:
  + Light green: low intensity
  + Medium green: medium intensity
  + Dark green: high intensity

In a CARTOFINDER™ map, the coloring corresponds to the color of the CARTOFINDER™ site electrodes.

All colored areas, including lighter gray coloring, display areas where acquisition has occurred.

![CARTOFINDER_ROIColoring_Map.png](data:image/png;base64...)

Figure 144: CARTOFINDER™ Map

FDA Request

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![DxNoteGeneric.png](data:image/png;base64...) | NOTE  **Identifying rotational activation patterns:**  The system uses the annotations detected by the algorithm to identify areas with a rotational activation pattern in which the following apply:   * The participating electrodes cover at least 50% of the local cycle length. * Spline space-criteria is based on the mechanical specifications of the mapping catheter. * There are at least 2 consecutive repetitions of the activation sequence.   **Identifying rotational activation patterns:**   * The unipolar electrogram has a QS pattern * This is the earliest QS pattern among neighboring electrodes * The QS pattern occurs for two or more consecutive beats |

FDA Request

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![DxNoteDanger.png](data:image/png;base64...) | **WARNING**  The CARTOFINDER™ Module is not intended as the sole means for identifying focal and rotational activation patterns. It is intended to aid the physician in the evaluation of atrial electrical activation. You should always review the local electrogram data. |

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![DxNoteCaution.png](data:image/png;base64...) | *CAUTION*   * The spatial resolution algorithm limitation in displaying rotational activities may depend on the type of catheter used (electrode size and density of electrodes). * Rotational drivers can occupy a large area and therefore may be difficult to map with sequential recording. The clinical significance of rotational activation has not been established. |

|  |  |

##### CARTOFINDER™ CL (Cycle Length) Map

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/CARTOFINDER\_cycle\_length\_map\_v7\_EN.xml

A CARTOFINDER™ CL map displays the dominant cycle length that is calculated for each channel in each CARTOFINDER™ site.

You can filter out electrodes where the calculated cycle length standard deviation (Std) is above a user-defined threshold. These electrodes project white on the map, instead of projecting color. The threshold is set in the CARTOFINDER™ tab of the Preferences dialog box.

A CARTOFINDER™ Dynamic map displays LAT values for each of the participating signals at each timestamp during playback, allowing you to view the activation propagation over time.

When the CARTOFINDER™ Dynamic map is selected, the following occurs:

* The CARTOFINDER™ Dynamic color scale bar appears (in Custom Color Scale).

To change the minimum and maximum LAT values in the displayed map, drag the sliders to the desired values.

##### Using the CARTOFINDER™ Dynamic Map Player

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/FINDER\_DisplayMap\_v7\_EN.xml

The CARTOFINDER™ map player allows you to view the activation propagation over time. CARTOFINDER™ Dynamic maps can be displayed in both the Main Map Viewer and the Additional Map Viewer.

The CARTOFINDER™ Dynamic map is colored according to the player timeline position in the Selected Point Viewer. Whenever the player timeline position corresponds to a detected annotation on a channel, the area around the specific electrode in the map turns red. As map play progresses, the area around the electrode turns gradually from red to blue, according to the CARTOFINDER™ Dynamic color scale bar.

You can drag the timeline to scroll beyond the border of the Selected Point Viewer.

![CARTOFINDER_LAT4DColoring_red.jpg](data:image/jpeg;base64...)

Figure 149: CARTOFINDER™ Dynamic Coloring Around a Single Electrode

Play automatically stops when a different CARTOFINDER™ site, point, contour, or map coloring type is selected.

If the CARTOFINDER™ Dynamic map type is selected, the play button is displayed.

![CARTOFINDER_PlayerPlayButton_LAT4D.jpg](data:image/jpeg;base64...)

Figure 150: CARTOFINDER™ Map Play Button

Hover over the ![FinderPlayerPlay.jpg](data:image/jpeg;base64...) to open the full map player with all of the player controls.

![CARTOFINDER_FullPlayer_LAT4D.jpg](data:image/jpeg;base64...)

Figure 151: CARTOFINDER™ Map Player- Expanded View

#### Recommended Clinical Workflow

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/Condor\_ClinicalWorkflow\_v7\_EN.xml

The following workflow recommendation is a general guidance on how to use the CARTO™ 3 System for mapping the left atrium (LA) in an atrial fibrillation (AF) case. This is a general recommendation. The sequence of steps may vary and additional mapping may be done.

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| 1. | Connect the PENTARAY™ NAV Catheter. |
| 2. | Create a FAM map of the LA (cardiac geometry), as described below:   * Set the FAM Resolution Level to 17 or higher. * Enable Respiration Gating. * Obtain a bipolar voltage map or another map, according to your common clinical practice's mapping procedure and voltage settings. * Use TPI for FAM and an apply the internal points filter for voltage and LAT points. * Apply Point Tags with the appropriate type for fractionation and signals of interest.  |  |  | | --- | --- | | ![DxNoteGeneric.png](data:image/png;base64...) | NOTE  It is important to finalize the FAM editing before acquiring additional EA data and CARTOFINDER™ recordings. This ensures optimal alignment of electrical information, such as voltage and CARTOFINDER™ recordings, for each electrode. | |
| 3. | Edit the map according to your typical clinical workflow. |
| 4. | Add anatomical structures according to your typical clinical workflow. |
| 5. | Isolate the pulmonary veins. |
| 6. | Create a new voltage map, focusing on the areas between the pulmonary veins:   * Position the PENTARAY™ NAV Catheter on the chamber wall. Ensure that the catheter is stable and that the splines are spread out evenly. * Use TPI visualization to assist with contact validation. |
| 7. | With the voltage map in one viewer, start a CARTOFINDER™ map in the other viewer. |
| 8. | Create CARTOFINDER™ recordings:   * Start the recording in a location where the catheter position is stable. * Recording stops automatically after 30 seconds. Delete and reacquire any recordings that are less than 20 seconds. * For each additional recording, position the distal electrode of the catheter overlapping the previous acquisition site electrode. * Complete acquisitions for the chamber. |
| 9. | Review CARTOFINDER™ sites, maps, and signals:   * Review the unipolar channels per CARTOFINDER™ site, ensuring annotations are properly marked and removing channels, if needed. (See ["Viewing CARTOFINDER™ Sites"](#target9000095).) * Ensure that the displayed CARTOFINDER™ maps are accurate and match the signal data. (See ["Viewing a CARTOFINDER™ Module Map"](#target9000096).) * Ensure that the additional data on the CARTO™ 3 System (voltage area or any specific tagging, such as CFAE, scar, and CL) is accurate and matches the signal data. |

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![DxNoteGeneric.png](data:image/png;base64...) | NOTE  Use your clinical judgement regarding the data displayed to make sure that the information is accurate. |

## COHERENT Mapping

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/CoherentOverview\_v7\_EN.xml

**Coherent Mapping** is a CARTO™ 3 System feature that improves the representation of the electric wave propagation over the atria by means of coloring and direction vectors, focusing on the displayed path of cyclic arrhythmia propagation. The reconstruction includes detection of areas with probable conduction barriers (slow or no conduction).

After a COHERENT map is calculated, you can display conduction velocity vectors, either static or dynamic, on any map type (see ["COHERENT Map Display Options"](#target9000097)).

FDA Req. No. K173977/S001 dated February 23, 2018.

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![DxNoteGeneric.png](data:image/png;base64...) | NOTE  The Coherent Mapping algorithm is designed to support cases of typical flutter and atypical flutter. Use of the algorithm was validated only in these conditions. |

REP9626 1.2.1, V7 Coherent

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![DxNoteDanger.png](data:image/png;base64...) | **WARNING**  Before applying energy for ablation, visually verify correctness of the tip position of the mapping catheter in relation to the visualized map. Use your common clinical practice for real-time verification of catheter locations throughout the procedure (inspection of IC signals, direct imaging guidance such as fluoroscopy or ultrasound, etc.). Failure to do so might result in incorrect placement of the catheter. |

![coherent_Overview_v7.png](data:image/png;base64...)

Figure 152: Coherent Map (Left) and LAT Map (Right)

### About the Coherent Mapping Algorithm

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/CoherentOverview\_algorithm\_v7\_EN.xml

FDA Req. No. K173977/S001 dated February 23, 2018.

The Coherent Mapping algorithm was designed based on the electrophysiological conditions of typical flutter, atypical flutter, and focal atrial tachycardia. In these conditions, conduction velocity is continuous, meaning that there are no sharp turns of wave velocity, except in areas with slow conduction or blocks with tissue properties as defined in the literature. The algorithm uses the Local Activation Time (LAT) values of all the points in a map and performs an iterative calculation for a global solution in these arrhythmias.

The main conditions used by the algorithm are:

* Low potential points such as scar
* Double-potential points
* Dense map of the chamber with uniform distribution of points

For optimal performance, we recommend:

* Scar settings are properly defined according to your clinical practice.
* Signals that you identify as double-potential are tagged as such.
* Map data must be dense enough to allow continuous interpolation of activation between the points.
* Anatomical structures are marked.

The iterative nature of the calculation leads to a global best-fit solution, analogous to calculating a regression line to show how scattered points indicate a global trend.

![coherent_map_diagram_v7.png](data:image/png;base64...)

Figure 153: LAT Coloring (Left) and Coherent Coloring (Right)

In the image above, LAT coloring presents a local solution, where every point has an individual contribution to the map coloring. Coherent coloring presents a global best-fit solution based on all the map points.

### Viewing a COHERENT Map

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/CoherentMap\_viewing\_v7\_EN.xml

COHERENT maps can be displayed in either map viewer.

When you first select the COHERENT map coloring type, the Coherent Mapping algorithm automatically calculates the coloring for the Active Map.

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![DxNoteGeneric.png](data:image/png;base64...) | NOTE  When more than one map is displayed, Coherent Coloring and Velocity Vectors must be calculated separately for each map. |

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| 1. | Right-click the map in the Map List or the map viewer and select COHERENT.  The System begins the calculation. The color scale bar changes to indicate progress.  ![coherent_progressScale.png](data:image/png;base64...)  The calculation time depends on the amount of data in the map. During this time, acquisition is disabled, and there might be a delay in the display of some functions (for example, VISITAG™ data).  If there is an error in the calculation, the map is displayed in gray. |
| 2. | To cancel the calculation, click ![coherent_progressScale_cancel.png](data:image/png;base64...).  The map is displayed in gray. |

When the calculation is complete, the map coloring is updated. The Color Scale bar changes to the Cyclic Color Scale.

![coherent_full_Scale.png](data:image/png;base64...)

Figure 154: COHERENT Cyclic Color Scale

The COHERENT cycle length is displayed. This is based on the median cycle length of the LAT points in the Active Map (not including points filtered out as internal points or LAT outliers).

#### Working with the Cyclic Color Scale

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/CoherentMap\_changeColor\_v7\_EN.xml

REP9626 1.2.3, wrong cycle length, v7 COHERENT

The Cyclic Color Scale is in Automatic mode by default. In this mode, the starting point is set at the earliest LAT value, according to the Coherent Mapping algorithm. The end point is the start point value plus the COHERENT cycle length. The place where the start and end points meet is marked with a slider.

You can adjust the end points of the Cyclic Color Scale by dragging the slider. An arrow indicates the original value.

![coherent_full_Scale_adjusted.png](data:image/png;base64...)

Figure 155: Adjusted Cyclic Color Scale

The map coloring is updated while the slider is dragged. Double-click the slider to return to the original value.

![coherent_full_changeScale.png](data:image/png;base64...)

Figure 156: COHERENT Maps with Different Color Scale

As with the standard color scale bar, the Cyclic Color Scale can be set manually by dragging the end points, or by entering custom values. To access these options, right-click the Cyclic Color Scale.

![coherent_adjustedColorScale_menu.png](data:image/png;base64...)

Figure 157: Color Scale Options

In Manual mode, there are two sliders, for earliest and latest activation. Drag the sliders to the desired location. The map coloring is updated accordingly, with red indicating the earliest activation. To return to the initial value, double-click a slider.

![coherent_adjustedColorScale_manual.png](data:image/png;base64...)

Figure 158: COHERENT Map with Manual Color Scale

In Custom mode, the default range is -200 to 200. To change, click the number and enter the new value.

![coherent_adjustedColorScale_custom.png](data:image/png;base64...)

Figure 159: COHERENT Map with Custom Color Scale

If you switch back to Automatic mode, the System remembers your last Manual and Custom values.

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![DxNoteGeneric.png](data:image/png;base64...) | NOTE   * Changes to the COHERENT color scale only affect the display of the COHERENT map. Actual LAT values are not changed. * Propagation map playback is available for both Automatic and Manual color scale modes. |

#### COHERENT Map Display Options

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/CoherentMap\_DisplayOptions\_v7p3\_EN.xml

You can control the way the COHERENT map is displayed. You can define different settings for each map, in each map viewer.

To open the dialog box, right-click the COHERENT map and select Coherent Visualization Setup.

Table 74: Coherent Visualization Setup Options

| Option | Description |
| --- | --- |
| Transparent Slow or No Conduction Zone | When selected, areas detected as zones with slow or no conduction (SNO zones) are semi-transparent.  ![coherent_ShowScar.png](data:image/png;base64...)  When not selected, areas detected as zones with slow or no conduction are opaque, and colored brown.  ![coherent_ShowScar_brown.png](data:image/png;base64...) |
| Conduction Velocity Vectors | When selected, vectors indicating conduction velocity and direction are displayed, for all map coloring types (e.g., LAT).  Conduction velocity is not displayed on anatomical structures, on identified probable conduction barriers, or in areas where there is not enough data.  ![coherent_vectors_v7.png](data:image/png;base64...)  Vectors on the Active Map are white. If vectors are displayed on additional maps, they are colored cyan.  ![coherent_vectors_multiMaps_v7.png](data:image/png;base64...) |

| Dynamic | When selected, the velocity vectors are animated.  Dynamic velocity vectors cannot be displayed during FAM acquisition.   * The vectors become static if you start FAM acquisition. * FAM acquisition stops if you activate Dynamic velocity vectors. |
| Threshold | The Threshold slider controls the appearance of the velocity vectors presented on the map.   * In areas of slower conduction (below the threshold), velocity vectors are thick. * In areas of faster conduction (above the threshold), velocity vectors are thin.   ![vector_display.png](data:image/png;base64...) |

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/CoherentMap\_changeCL\_v7\_EN.xml

### Viewing Multiple COHERENT Maps

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/CoherentMap\_multipleMaps\_v7\_EN.xml

When the COHERENT map coloring type is selected, the following considerations apply:

* The Coherent Mapping calculation only affects the Active Map.
  + If there are additional maps in the map viewer that were not calculated, they are displayed in gray.
  + If there are additional maps in the map viewer that changed since the last calculation, a message appears (see ["Recalculating the COHERENT Map"](#target9000098)).
  + Run the calculation for each map separately.
* The COHERENT cycle length presented below the Cyclic Color Scale applies only to the Active Map.
* The LAT range applies to all maps in the viewer.
* Propagation map playback applies to all maps in the Main Map Viewer.

### Recalculating the COHERENT Map

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/CoherentMap\_refresh\_v7p3\_EN.xml

REP9626 1.2.5, map not up to date, v7 COHERENT

If the map changes after the calculation, a recalculate button appears in the COHERENT map, and the map coloring type appears in red. If the conduction velocity vectors are displayed (in any map type), a message appears. You must click the button in the COHERENT map to repeat the calculation.

![coherent_reset.png](data:image/png;base64...)

Figure 162: Recalculation Indication: Single Map

![coherent_recalculate_multiMaps.png](data:image/png;base64...)

Figure 163: Recalculation Indication: Multiple Maps

The previous coloring and conduction velocity indication is displayed until the COHERENT map is recalculated.

Recalculation is required after the following changes:

* Acquiring or deleting FAM data, changing the resolution, including or ignoring EA points
* Acquiring, deleting, or editing points
* Including or excluding points using the Internal Points Filter or Map Consistency activation
* Copying or moving points to the map
* Tagging or untagging a point as a type (Normal, Location Only, Floating, Scar)
* User-defined cycle length change
* Creating, deleting, or modifying an anatomical structure
* Assigning or updating ultrasound contours in a way that affects the anatomy

During the calculation, the Acquisition panel is disabled.

## Parallel Mapping

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/parallel-LAT\_UsingPara\_EN.xml

**Parallel Mapping** can be used for continuous mapping of up to 4 maps with different map settings and different acquisition filters. For example, different acquisition filters can be used for different settings, such as cycle length, signal morphology, etc. In addition, acquired data can be recalculated retrospectively according to different acquisition filters.

* **Real-time acquisition**: Use Parallel Mapping to create a group up to 4 maps and assign each one a different set of acquisition filters. During continuous acquisition, EA points are acquired to the appropriate map.
* **Recalculate an existing map**: The Map Replay option creates a new map by recalculating an existing map according to different acquisition filters.
* **Retrospectively acquire points from a specific time in the study**: Define a time frame in the CARTOREPLAY™ Module Timeline and acquire points according to the acquisition filters in the current Active Map.

This feature relies on continuous mapping acquisition filters. For details, refer to the *CONFIDENSE™ Module User Guide*.

### Performing Parallel Mapping

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/parallel-LAT\_Para\_live\_v7p3\_EN.xml

Real-time Parallel Mapping is used to create a group of up to 4 maps with different map settings and different acquisition filters. When Continuous Mapping is active, points are acquired to each map according to its own filters.

Any maps can be added to a group, including maps created by Save Map As, Remap, and Map Replay.

Requirements for grouping:

* All maps in the group must have the same Body Coordinate System (BCS).
* All maps in the group must have the same Chamber of Interest, acquisition mode (Gated or Stable), and Reference (both Channel and Criterion).

All other parameters in the Map Setup screen and the Acquisition Filters preferences can be different.

A Parallel Mapping group can contain a mix of standard and LAT Hybrid maps, as long as all maps in the group are ventricular.

All maps in the group share the same FAM data. For details, see ["Creating a Group When There Is Existing FAM Data"](#target9000099).

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![DxNoteGeneric.png](data:image/png;base64...) | NOTE   * For maps in the same group, FAM acquisition does not stop when switching the active map to FAM another map. * For maps in different groups, FAM acquisition will stop when switching the active map to a map in a different group. |

REP9626 1.3.4, v7 parallel mapping, clear names

You can set up each map before beginning acquisition or create each one when conditions change. Ensure that each map name is clear enough to identify the main reason for the filter (such as cycle length or arrhythmia type).

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| 1. | Create the first map with the required Map Setup parameters and acquisition filters.  The Preferences dialog box can be opened from the Map Setup screen.  ![map_setup_acqfiltersv8p3.png](data:image/png;base64...) |
| 2. | Create one or more additional maps with the appropriate map settings and acquisition filters.  This can be done before or after points are acquired to the first map. |
| 3. | In the Map List of the Main Map Viewer, right-click a map and select Parallel Mapping and then select the appropriate map from the submenu.  ![para_mapList_menu.png](data:image/png;base64...)   |  |  | | --- | --- | | ![para_acqPanel_fam-CM_Active.png](data:image/png;base64...) | The images in the Continuous Mapping and FAM buttons are doubled to show that multiple maps are affected. | | ![parallel_mapList_group.png](data:image/png;base64...) | Map names in the group are colored orange. Only one map from the group can be displayed at a time. | |
| 4. | To add another map to the group, right-click the map and select Parallel Mapping.  ![parallel_map_ParaNotSelected.png](data:image/png;base64...) |
| 5. | Start acquisition.  Use the Filters Status toolbar to monitor progress in each map. (See ["Filters Status Toolbar in Parallel Mapping"](#target9000100).)  ![parallel_filter_status_collapsed.png](data:image/png;base64...) |
| 6. | To remove a map from the group, right-click the map and clear Parallel Mapping. To break the group, right-click one of the maps in the group and select Ungroup All Maps.  ![para_mapList_menu_paraSelected.png](data:image/png;base64...)  The map retains all data that was acquired while it was part of the group.   |  |  | | --- | --- | | ![DxNoteGeneric.png](data:image/png;base64...) | NOTE  If you change a group (add or remove a map) during acquisition, acquisition stops. | |
| 7. | End acquisition as usual. |

**Considerations:**

* Automatically acquired EA points (continuous mapping) are acquired to the appropriate map or maps, according to the filters and map parameters. The anatomy of each map depends on the acquired points.
* Manually acquired points, ultrasound contours, and CARTOFINDER™ sites are acquired only to the active map.
* FAM data is shared by all maps in the group. If the FAM reconstruction is edited (shaving, erasing, changing resolution, and so on), changes affect all maps in the group.
* Any FAM-EA maps have a unique reconstruction, based on the EA points in the given map.
* If a map reaches the maximum number of points, or the maximum points in a study is reached, continuous acquisition is stopped.

**Anatomical structures:**

* When adding a FAM map to a group, anatomical structures applied to the map before the map is added to a group are applied to each FAM map in the group.
* When grouping FAM maps with FAM-EA maps that have anatomical differences, the anatomical structure is projected onto the revised anatomy.
* Point-by-Point maps added to a group retain their original anatomical structure.
* Edits made to anatomical structures on FAM maps that are part of a group, are applied to all FAM maps in the group. If there is a difference in the anatomy, the anatomical structure is projected onto the other FAM maps in the group.
* Creating or deleting anatomical structures on a FAM map in a group affects all the FAM maps in the group.
* Edits made to anatomical structures on Point-by-Point maps are not applied to any other maps in the group.

REP9626 1.3.1, v7 parallel mapping ablation data

**Ablation data:**

* Ablation sessions are associated with each map in the group.
* Ablation tags are duplicated in each map in the group. A comment is added in the Point List, indicating the map that was active when the point was acquired.
* VISITAG™ Sites are displayed in each map.

#### Filters Status Toolbar in Parallel Mapping

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/parallel\_filter-status\_EN.xml

The Filters Status toolbar has separate tabs for each map.

The tabs display the map name and the pattern number. If a map name is too long, the numerical prefix is removed. For example, map 1-1-1-Rp-PVCA appears as Rp-PVCA. If there is a LAT Hybrid map, the Activation pattern is displayed.

The color of the tab's lower border indicates the status: green if the point passes all the filters, and red if it does not.

![parallel_filter_status.png](data:image/png;base64...)

Figure 164: Filters Status Toolbar with Parallel Mapping Group

When the toolbar opens, the tab for the Active Map is selected.

* To view the status for a different map, click the tab.
* To change the acquisition filters for a different map, click the filter icon.

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![DxNoteGeneric.png](data:image/png;base64...) | NOTE  Changing the displayed map in the Filters Status toolbar does not change the Active Map. |

#### Creating a Group When There Is Existing FAM Data

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/parallel-LAT\_livePara\_group\_FAM\_EN.xml

There are some special considerations when creating or adding to a group when there is existing FAM data.

| Action | Result |
| --- | --- |
| Adding a map with FAM data to a map or group without FAM data | The existing FAM data is copied to the group. |
| Adding a FAM map with no FAM data to a group with FAM data | The existing FAM data is copied to the new map. |
| Adding a map with FAM to a group with FAM data | The FAM for the group replaces the FAM data in the added map.  A confirmation message appears. You have the option to cancel the grouping and retain the FAM data. |
| Creating a group with 2 maps which both have FAM data | The FAM data in the map that is selected from the submenu replaces the FAM in the map that was originally selected in the right-click menu.  A confirmation message appears. You have the option to cancel the grouping and retain the FAM data.  REP9626 1.3.5, v7 parallel mapping FAM functionality |
| Creating a group with 2 FAM maps when only one has FAM data | The existing FAM data is copied to the empty map, regardless of selection order |

### Using Map Replay to Recalculate a Map

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/parallel-LAT\_Para\_rebuild\_EN.xml

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| 1. | Select Map > Map Replay.  The Map Setup screen appears. The prefix Rp- is added to the map name. |
| 2. | Adjust settings as needed.  The following settings can be changed:   * Reference * Map Type: FAM can be recalculated as Point-by-Point * LAT Hybrid |
| 3. | Set the acquisition filters.  Click the Filter button in the Map Setup screen to access the Preferences dialog box.  ![map_setup_acqfiltersv8p3.png](data:image/png;base64...) |
| 4. | Click Calculate.  If the BCS for part of the map does not match the current BCS, you will be prompted to either continue or cancel the calculation.  Progress indication appears while the process is active. |

**Considerations:**

* The new map is generated with electrical data from the time when the original map was active or part of an active group. The starting point for the calculation is the timestamp of first data acquired in the map (FAM or EA). This includes data added with the CARTOREPLAY™ Module when the original map was not active.
* When the original map is a FAM map:
  + The FAM volume is copied to the new map (similar to Remap). FAM-EA points from the original maps are not included.
  + EA points are added to the new map according to the new acquisition filters.
  + The map can be recalculated as a Point-by-Point map instead of FAM.
* When the original map is a Point-by-Point map, points are added and anatomy is recalculated according to the new acquisition filters.
* The map data is evaluated from the beginning of acquisition. Stopping the calculation does not cancel the acquisition. Instead, points that were already identified are added to the map.

**Additional map data:**

* Tagged points are copied as Location Only points (similar to Remap).
* CARTOFINDER™ Module sites are not copied.

**Ablation data:**

* Ablation sessions from the original map are duplicated, and ablation tags are copied to the recalculated map.
* The acquisition filters are not applied for the duration of any ablation sessions in the original map.
* VISITAG™ Sites are displayed in the recalculated map.

### Continuous Mapping with CARTOREPLAY™ Module

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/parallel-LAT\_Para\_Replay\_EN.xml

You can retroactively build a new map or add data to an existing map using the CARTOREPLAY™ Module. Acquisition is according to acquisition filters in the current Active Map, with the data acquired during the user-defined acquisition time frame.

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| 1. | If necessary, use the Timeline Monitor to retroactively capture body surface signals.  ![replay_IS_v7.png](data:image/png;base64...)  Intracardiac signals cannot be captured retroactively. |
| 2. | In the Map List, make sure that the target map is active.  You can create a new map, if needed. |
| 3. | Set the acquisition preferences for the Active Map. |
| 4. | In Timeline mode, scroll to the time of interest. |
| 5. | Right-click the Timeline and select Continuous Mapping Start.  ![para_retro_options_start.png](data:image/png;base64...)  The acquisition time frame is displayed.  ![para_retro_start_Range.png](data:image/png;base64...)  The retroactive acquisition button changes from manual to continuous. |
| 6. | To set the end of the acquisition time frame, right-click the Timeline and select Continuous Mapping End.  You can also adjust the time frame by dragging the borders. |
| 7. | Click the Timeline continuous acquisition button.  ![para_retro_acquiButton.png](data:image/png;base64...)  If the BCS for acquisition time frame does not match the current BCS, you will be prompted to either continue or cancel the acquisition.  A progress bar appears. Points are acquired to the Active Map, according to the current acquisition filters. |

**Considerations:**

* If the Active Map at the selected time was part of a group, points are acquired to all maps in the group. A message appears, allowing you to cancel the retrospective acquisition if you want to break the group and add the retrospective data only to Active Map.
* If a map reaches the maximum number of points, or the maximum points in a study is reached, retroactive stops and the acquisition button is disabled.
* Continuous mapping is not available during ablation. The acquisition filters are not applied for the duration of any ablation sessions that occurred during the acquisition time frame.
* The acquisition filters are applied chronologically to the time frame. Stopping the retroactive mapping process does not cancel the acquisition. Instead, points that were already identified are added to the map.
* FAM acquisition is not available in the CARTOREPLAY™ Module.

If the Active Map of the selected time frame is a FAM map, the CARTOREPLAY™ points will be projected on the map's original FAM anatomy. If no anatomy is available, points will appear floating. Before acquiring points with the CARTOREPLAY™ Module, it is recommended that you create a re-map anatomy.

## LAT Hybrid Maps

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/parallel-LAT\_UsingLAT\_EN.xml

Transient changes in the heart rhythm, such as PVC, can lead to catheter displacement compared to normal sinus activation. **LAT Hybrid** maps provide a solution for PVC mapping by creating a hybrid point: the coloring reflects the LAT value during PVC rhythm, and the location corresponds to the location during sinus rhythm.

This feature relies on Continuous Mapping acquisition filters. For details, refer to the *CONFIDENSE™ Module User Guide*.

### Setting Up a LAT Hybrid Map

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/parallel-LAT\_createLat-H\_v7p3\_EN.xml

The LAT Hybrid option is available only for ventricular maps. The LAT Hybrid option does not depend on map type (Point-by-Point or FAM) or acquisition mode (Gated or Stable).

|  |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 1. | Start a new map.   |  |  | | --- | --- | | ![DxNoteGeneric.png](data:image/png;base64...) | NOTE  You cannot use the Quick Map workflow to create a LAT Hybrid map. | |
| 2. | Select LAT Hybrid.  The Chamber of Interest option is disabled. The LAT Hybrid option can be changed until the first point acquisition.  ![Setup_map_LAT-hybrid.png](data:image/png;base64...) |
| 3. | Capture both the PVC pattern and the sinus rhythm pattern. In the Pattern Bank, select the appropriate signal type (PVC, V SR).  ![patternBank_PVC_options.png](data:image/png;base64...) |
| 4. | Set the acquisition filters. You can do this in the Preferences dialog box or the Filters Status toolbar.  There are some changes to the filters in a LAT Hybrid map:   |  |  | | --- | --- | | Pattern Matching: PVC | Select the PVC pattern. | | Pattern Matching: NSR | Select the sinus pattern. | | Position Stability | Evaluates catheter stability. This filter is important for the effectiveness of the LAT Hybrid map. The default value is 4 mm. | | Density | Set to maximum. This cannot be changed. | | Cycle Length | Not available. |   The remaining filters behave the same as in standard Continuous Mapping. |
| 5. | Begin mapping.  For best results, ensure that the catheter is stable during mapping. |

The System evaluates the filters differently for manual acquisition and automatic continuous mapping. See the following sections for details.

![LAT-hybrid_status_filters_toolbar.png](data:image/png;base64...)

Figure 165: LAT Hybrid Filters in Filters Status Toolbar

#### Evaluation of Automatically Acquired Points

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/parallel-LAT\_automatic\_LAT\_EN.xml

When Continuous Mapping is active, the System automatically evaluates each potential point against the acquisition filters. The sequence below describes the process.

* Find a potential PVC point, according to the Activation pattern.
* Confirm that the previous beat matches the normal sinus rhythm, according to the Location pattern.
* The Position Stability and LAT Stability filters are applied to two consecutive sinus beats prior to the PVC beat.
* Evaluate remaining filters on the sinus beat.

If any active filter fails, the point is discarded.

In a LAT Hybrid map, the Filters Status toolbar is updated to show both the PVC and NSR filter status. To view either pattern in the pattern viewer, click the status box.

![LAT-H_filters_notActive.png](data:image/png;base64...)

Figure 166: Filters Status Toolbar in a LAT Hybrid Map

#### Manually Acquired Points

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/parallel-LAT\_manual\_LAT\_EN.xml

LAT Hybrid points can be acquired manually.

After you acquire a point and select the PVC beat in the beat buffer, the System searches the 5 beats prior to the selected PVC beat for a sinus beat based on the Location pattern. If no sinus beat is found, the PVC beat is also assigned as the location beat. This point will be marked in the point list as a non-hybrid point.

If the Respiration Gated filter is active, the System searches for an end-expiration sinus beat within 5 beats prior to the PVC beat. If no match is found, the PVC point is assigned as a non-hybrid point.

If the Tissue Proximity filter is active, it is applied to the sinus beat.

Non-hybrid points are marked with a red cross in the map. They are floating points, which do not affect map anatomy or coloring. The type can be changed through the point's right-click menu.

If a point is acquired manually during ablation, activation and location will be assigned to the same beat. The Ablation tag is applied.

### Display of LAT Hybrid Points

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/parallel-LAT\_display\_EN.xml

Each point acquired in the LAT Hybrid map is a combination of 2 beats. The location data comes from the sinus beat. All other data (activation, ECG signals, force values) from the PVC beat.

Catheter visualization is according to the sinus position.

When showing the catheter positions for a selected point, the location at the sinus beat is used.

In the Point List, the distance between the sinus rhythm location and the PVC location is displayed in the Hybrid Distance column. If there are points which are not hybrid (for example, manually acquired or copied from another map), the comment Non-Hybrid Point is added.

![lat-hybrid_pointList.png](data:image/png;base64...)

Figure 167: LAT Hybrid Points in the Point List

In the Selected Point Viewer, signals for the PVC beat are displayed.

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![DxNoteGeneric.png](data:image/png;base64...) | NOTE  To assess the catheter location during the PVC beat, double-click the point in Timeline mode. The selected point in the map is in the sinus location. |

### Interactions with Other Maps

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/parallel-LAT\_lat-otherMaps\_EN.xml

Hybrid points can be copied or moved to other maps.

* When both source and target are LAT Hybrid maps, the PVC and sinus data is retained.

REP9626 1.4.1, v7 LAT haybrid

* When the maps are not the same type, you must confirm the move. If you continue, the point's sinus location is retained in the target map.

A Parallel Mapping group can contain a mix of standard and LAT Hybrid maps, as long as all maps in the group are ventricular.

The Map Replay option can be used to create a LAT Hybrid map from a non-LAT Hybrid map, or the other way around.

## Map Merge

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/c3\_Map\_Merge\_EN.xml

**Map Merge** is an enhanced way to load maps from a previous study with VISITAG™ and ablation data, and view them together with current study maps. Using maps from previous studies in a current active map allows you to navigate the catheter to areas of interest from previous ablations and identify areas where the study may need to be re-evaluated.

In the Main Map Viewer, the imported map is displayed as a static (brown) layer with previous ablation data, including VISITAG™ Sites, also displayed in brown. In the Additional Map Viewer, the imported map is displayed on a brown background.

![map_merge_overview.jpg](data:image/jpeg;base64...)

Figure 168: Viewing Merged Maps

REP9626: 1.10.6 V7 Map Merge

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![DxNoteGeneric.png](data:image/png;base64...) | NOTE  Maps loaded from a previous study represent the anatomy with the data at the time of the acquisition, and do not reflect changes since that time, which may result in inaccurate registration. |

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![DxNoteCaution.png](data:image/png;base64...) | *CAUTION*  Use your common clinical practice for real-time verification of catheter locations throughout the procedure (inspection of IC signals, direct imaging guidance such as fluoroscopy or ultrasound, etc.). Failure to do so might result in incorrect placement of the catheter.  The information provided by these maps is for diagnostic purposes only. Therapeutic treatment should rely on all available information in the EP lab. |

### Loading Maps from a Previous Study

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/c3\_Map\_Merge\_Load\_Map\_EN.xml

REP9626: 1.10.12 V7 Map Merge

REP9626: 1.10.4 V7 Map Merge for step 2 - Patient Details Mismatch

Before loading a map from a previous study into a current study map, open the previous study map in Review Mode and set the VISITAG™ presets. Edit the map anatomy where needed, and then exit Review Mode.

All VISITAG™ and ablation information is based on the presets from the original study.

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| 1. | Start a new study and enter the Patient Details (Study Setup). |
| 2. | Select Study > Load Maps From Previous Study.  The selection dialog box opens.   |  |  | | --- | --- | | ![DxNoteGeneric.png](data:image/png;base64...) | NOTE  You cannot load maps from a previous study after the current study is initialized. | |
| 3. | Select the patient and study from the list.  If the patient details for the selected study are not identical to the patient details for the current study, a message about the mismatch is displayed.  ![map_merge_patient_details_mismatch.png](data:image/png;base64...)  To load the selected study in spite of the mismatch, click Continue. |
| 4. | Click Import. |
| 5. | Continue setting up the study and move to the Mapping phase.  The maps from the previous study are loaded into the Additional Map Viewer in color, with a brown background.  By default, the maps are hidden in the Main Map Viewer. To display them, select them from the Map List. Maps from a previous study are displayed as a static brown image. |
| 6. | Use the Previous Map Toolbar to set the display options in either map viewer (see ["Previous Map Toolbar"](#target9000101)). |

After acquiring map data in the new study, the new map(s) can be merged with maps from the previous study. See ["Registering Maps from a Previous Study"](#target9000102).

#### Display in the Main Map Viewer

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/c3\_Map\_Merge\_Visualization\_EN.xml

When viewing maps from previous studies, information from the previous study is displayed in the Main Map Viewer as a static brown layer. The following information is displayed in the Main Map Viewer:

REP9626: 1.10.7 V7 Map Merge

* Current VISITAG™ and ablation tags
* Previous VISITAG™ and ablation tags - as a static brown layer that cannot be edited
* Cumulative Data Popup window
* Catheters (current study only)

REP9626: 1.10.3 V7 Map Merge

Maps from Previous Studies are Read-Mode only, and cannot be edited. Maps from previous studies cannot be selected as Active Maps for acquiring points.

#### Display in the Additional Map Viewer

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/c3\_Map\_Merge\_Visitag\_EN.xml

When viewing maps from previous studies in the Additional Map Viewer, the following information is displayed:

REP9626: 1.10.10 V7 Map Merge

* Previous ablation tags and VISITAG™ Sites in color
* Previous points and tagged points in color
* Previous Map toolbar
* Cumulative Data Popup window

![prev_map_visitag.png](data:image/png;base64...)

Figure 169: Maps from Previous Studies in the Additional Map Viewer

When viewing a map from a previous study in the Additional Map Viewer:

* Current maps cannot be displayed in the Additional Map Viewer.
* Real-time catheters are not displayed in the Additional Map Viewer.
* The map reconstruction is not editable.

REP9626: 1.10.7 V7 Map Merge

* VISITAG™ Preferences from the previous study are not available.

#### Previous Map Toolbar

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/c3\_Map\_Merge\_Visitag\_Toolbar\_EN.xml

REP9626: 1.10.11 V7 Map Merge

The Previous Map toolbar is used to set the display options for VISITAG™ and ablation information for maps from previous studies in both the Main Map Viewer and the Additional Map Viewer.

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![DxNoteGeneric.png](data:image/png;base64...) | NOTE  You cannot edit any VISITAG™ preferences for maps from previous studies. |

### Registering Maps from a Previous Study

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/c3\_Map\_Merge\_Registration\_EN.xml

To register maps from a previous study into a current study, perform the following steps:

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| 1. | Create a new map or maps. |
| 2. | Right-click the map in the Main Map Viewer or the Map List and select Match Previous Map. |
| 3. | Select the map from the drop-down list.  The map is associated with the previous map. The loaded map is hidden by default (unselected. Select the Prev Map (prev) in the Map list to make it visible. |
| 4. | If you will use landmark registration, create landmarks on both the new map and the previous map.  You can perform landmark registration with either one landmark pair or multiple landmark pairs (at least 3). Each landmark pair marks the same position on both the new and the previous map. |
| 5. | Use the commands on the Registration Toolbar to register the map. |

When selected, unregistered maps are clearly indicated with a red border and UNREGISTERED.Prev beneath the Registration Toolbar.

![map_merge_unregistered_map.jpg](data:image/jpeg;base64...)

BR86755\_Sync map

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![DxNoteGeneric.png](data:image/png;base64...) | NOTE  Movements and rotations to the map from a previous study during the registration process are not reflected in the Additional Map Viewer. |

# Error Messages and Alerts

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/ErrorMessages\_intro\_c3-v4-3\_EN.xml

REP4611 PL.21 Error 105; PL.32 Error 18, PL.33 Errors 61001,61005 and 61401; D-V1-2.5.11; D-V1-2.5.9

During a study, the following types of messages might appear:

* **Error Messages**: These messages report an error state that limits the functionality of the system. You can continue using the system, but acquisition will be disabled and other functions might not be fully operational until you correct the error.
* **Alerts**: These messages report that something in the system requires attention. Alerts may require an action, but they do not interrupt the course of the procedure.
* **Info Messages**: These messages provide information about the current status, but they do not interrupt the course of the procedure.

The System Status icon indicates whether there is a message requiring your attention. If there is, the message number is displayed near the icon.

![error_icon_v4.png](data:image/png;base64...)

Figure 171: System Status Icon

The message display window can be displayed or hidden. If the window is hidden, click the System Status icon to open the message summary.

![Message_0002_v4.png](data:image/png;base64...)

Figure 172: Error Message Summary

Click Details to view the full message.

The message window remains open until you close it, even if there is no error or alert message. If you close the message window, it will remain closed even if a new message appears.

See also:

* ["System Status"](#target9000103)

## System Messages

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/SystemMessages\_c3-v6\_EN.xml

In some cases, a popup system message appears (for example, when copying points to a map with a different cycle length, or if leakage current is detected). If this occurs, you must follow the instructions in the popup message before continuing.

## Viewing Error Message Translations

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/Error\_Message\_translations\_c3-v4\_EN.xml

Error and alert message translations are available as screen tips for some languages.

When this option is enabled, translations of the message summary and message details appear when the mouse pointer is placed on the error message.

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![DxNoteGeneric.png](data:image/png;base64...) | NOTE  When Chinese is selected, the full Graphical User Interface (GUI) is translated. This is available after restarting the CARTO™ 3 System Software. |

REP4611 A-V3-27.8.1.1

The error and alert messages are included in the translations of the IFU on the Documentation CD that was supplied with this system.

## Catheter-Related Messages

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/catheter\_messages\_C3\_v4\_EN.xml

Catheter-related messages might be generated as a result of problems with any of the following:

* Connections between catheters and the PIU
* Catheter extension cables
* Catheters

If a catheter-related message is generated, determine the source of the problem before replacing a catheter.

## Message Text

https://bwiildita.sharepoint.com/sites/prod/Topics/system\_messages\_C3\_v8p3\_EN.xml

Messages are numbered. This number is used by Biosense Webster Service and Support to identify the specific type of problem. When contacting the Biosense Webster Service and Support Department, be sure to report the message number, as well as the circumstances in which the problem was encountered.

For any problems not mentioned in this list, contact the Biosense Webster Service and Support Department.

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![DxNoteGeneric.png](data:image/png;base64...) | NOTE  Messages marked with an asterisk (\*) are errors if they apply to the mapping catheter; otherwise, they are alerts. |

Table 76: System-Related Messages

| Message | Action |
| --- | --- |
| Error 1  No communication with PIU detected. | Perform the following until resolved:  1. Verify that the PIU is correctly connected to the power supply and that the power supply is ON.  2. Wait one minute to ensure that the PIU has finished its power-up process.  3. Verify that the optical cable is correctly connected between the workstation and the PIU.  4. Switch the PIU Power Supply off and on again, and wait few minutes for communication to be established between the PIU and the workstation. |
| Error 2  The PIU is initializing. | This process may take up to 7 minutes. |
| Error 3  The PIU is initializing. | PIU is testing the data flow integrity. This test may take one minute. |
| Error 4  The PIU Loader is not updated. | The Loader has to be updated by Biosense Webster Service and Support Department. |
| Error 5  Multiple ablation generators are connected. | Disconnect one of the generators to continue with CARTO™ 3 System initialization. |
| Error 7  Leakage current detected on PIU RL input. | Perform the following to determine if the problem was caused by one of the catheters or their extension cables, by the Body Surface ECG cable, or by the PIU:  1. Immediately disconnect Body Surface ECG cable, and  all catheter extension cables from the PIU.  2. Press Reconnect in the Caution window, to enable ablation through the PIU.  3. If the error persists, shut down the PIU and contact Biosense Webster Service and Support Department.  4. If the error message has disappeared, reconnect the Body Surface ECG cable, extension cables and catheters, one by one, until catheter or cable causing the leakage is identified.  5. Replace the faulty item.  6. If the error returns during ablation, disconnect all catheter cables and the Body Surface ECG cable from the PIU, turn off the PIU Power Supply, and contact Biosense Webster Service and Support Department to report a PIU issue. |
| Error 8  Stimulation routing is disabled. | An error was detected in the Stimulation routing hardware of the PIU. When possible, perform the following to determine if the problem was caused by the PIU, or by one of the catheters or their extension cables:  1. Disconnect all catheters and catheter extension cables from the PIU sockets.  2. Switch the PIU Power Supply off and on again.  3. Wait a few minutes for communication to be established between the PIU and the workstation.  4. If the problem persists, contact Biosense Webster Service and Support Department.  5. If the problem has disappeared, reconnect each cable and catheter, one by one, and check if the error re-appears after a particular cable or catheter connection. |
| Error 9  PIU Channel calibration error. | Accuracy of the CARTO™ 3 System may be compromised when this error appears.  Perform the following:  1. Switch off the Power Supply to the PIU, disconnect the cables connected to the REF/DECA, 20 POLE A and 20 POLE B sockets of the PIU, and switch the Power Supply on again.  2. Wait a few minutes for communication to be established between the PIU and the workstation.  3. If the error disappeared, reconnect the cables to the PIU and continue.  4. If the problem persists, contact Biosense Webster Service and Support Department. |
| Error 10  PIU initialization failure. | Perform the following:  1. Switch the Power Supply to the PIU off and on again.  2. Wait a few minutes for communication to be established between the PIU and the workstation.  3. If the problem persists, contact Biosense Webster Service and Support Department. |
| Error 13  ECG cards are not of same version. | ECG cards of same version must be installed by Biosense Webster Service and Support Department. |
| Error 14  PIU firmware has to be reloaded. Restart the PIU. | The loaded PIU firmware version is not compatible with the ACL Tx card. Switch the PIU power supply off and on again. Upon restart, the compatible PIU firmware version will be automatically loaded. |
| Error 17  Location Pad currents unstable. | Perform the following:  1. Switch off the PIU power supply.  2. Disconnect and re-connect the Location Pad to the PIU.  3. Disconnect and re-connect the Location Pad extension cable and switch on the power supply.  4. If the problem persists, contact Biosense Webster Service and Support Department. |
| Alert 18  The PIU Temperature is too high. | Perform the following:  1. Ensure that the PIU is properly ventilated.  2. Switch off the PIU power and let the PIU cool down, for a few minutes.  3. Switch on the power supply. If the problem persists, contact Biosense Webster Service and Support Department. |
| Error 19  Failure in PIU ECG1 card BIT. | Perform the following:  1. Switch the Power Supply to the PIU off and on again.  2. Wait a few minutes for communication to be established between the PIU and the workstation.  3. If the problem persists, contact Biosense Webster Service and Support Department. |
| Error 20  Failure in PIU ECG2 card BIT. | Perform the following:  1. Switch the Power Supply to the PIU off and on again.  2. Wait a few minutes for communication to be established between the PIU and the workstation.  3. If the problem persists, contact Biosense Webster Service and Support Department. |
| Error 21  Failure in PIU ECG3 card BIT. | Perform the following:  1. Switch the Power Supply to the PIU off and on again.  2. Wait a few minutes for communication to be established between the PIU and the workstation.  3. If the problem persists, contact Biosense Webster Service and Support Department. |
